# Skills · T · part 111

1,116 entries, to-prd to to-tickets. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **to-prd**: Turn the current conversation context or research&intent into a PRD (business/product contract) — objective, problem, solution, scope, user stories, and functional/non-functional requirements — without the technical implementation contract. Use when user wants a PRD, wants the business contract split from technical solutioning, or explicitly says the technical detail (architecture, APIs, data models, security, performance) will be captured separately in specs/design/decisions. ([dtriepke/skills](https://github.com/dtriepke/skills/tree/HEAD/packages/rafiki/.apm/skills/to-prd))
- **to-prd**: Turn the aligned task conversation and targeted repository evidence into a short-lived PRD under .agent-state/current-task/PRD.md. - **to-prd**: Turn current conversation context into a PRD and publish it to the project issue tracker — synthesize, do NOT interview. Use when the user wants to create a PRD from what was just discussed. For breaking that PRD into issues use `to-issues`; for triaging existing issues use `triage`; for design direction on a specific feature use `shape`. ([ekasc/skills](https://github.com/ekasc/skills/tree/HEAD/to-prd))
